## 2026-11-22 — G5 media restore destination alias guard + real Render disk handoff (source accepted, production offsite still missing)

- **Real existing-target overwrite flaw repaired in source:** The G5 encrypted catalog media CLI's isolated restore preflight previously used `fs.existsSync(target)`; Node returns false for a **dangling symlink**, allowing a later `renameSync` to replace its path. [Fix `30f451b6`](https://github.com/nora953/fawri-production-source/commit/30f451b60e1c011ceed16630fe211905aca3847b) checks `lstatSync(..., {throwIfNoEntry:false})` at both restore preflight and final rename guard, refusing existing filesystem entries even if the target is a broken symlink. [Test `8d04c1f8`](https://github.com/nora953/fawri-production-source/commit/8d04c1f8cf89395edec8266d2387b0167a3c6174) verifies the dangling symlink is **not overwritten** and the original authenticated archive remains restorable.

This is a narrower real candidate-media-source safety gate. No production Render deploy, shell, merchant data, Neon write, or offsite media transfer occurred.
- **Provider documented blocker narrowed precisely:** Render's [persistent disks documentation](https://render.com/docs/disks) confirms mounted disk files are accessible **only to the running service instance**, not GitHub build jobs, Render pre-deploy, another service, an ephemeral shell or a one-off job. Render disk snapshots are automatic daily with **seven-day retention**, are same-provider and restore a whole disk by overwriting later changes — **not independent offsite backup**. Secure export must originate in the live web service Shell or authorized SSH, then encrypted ciphertext can be transferred via SFTP/SCP or approved other channel. The connected Render plugin exposes neither Shell nor file export/read, so it **cannot** perform this final step autonomously.
- **Concrete single-owner execution packet created:** [`P0-V1-G5-RENDER-MEDIA-OFFSITE-HANDOFF-20261122.md`](docs/final-audit/evidence/P0-V1-G5-RENDER-MEDIA-OFFSITE-HANDOFF-20261122.md) pins real service/disk/media directory; stops before any operation if the G5 CLI isn't in the **live** container or the media root is absent; requires specific owner consent for a controlled maintenance-ON deploy of the accepted source **before** running the tool; and requires separate consent and independent offsite custody for read-only media export. The CLI is only in the **CI-built** newer Docker image, not on the currently LIVE Render SHA `9eed007943e1d93d960f360723b8ce7becc8f93a`. This packet is an instruction, not an authorization to touch mounted photos.

## 2026-11-22 — G5 media archive overwrite data-loss fixed; hosted G3 image RLS catalog confirmed (NO-GO)

- **New objectively real defect fixed in first-commercial encrypted media export candidate:** [`8cd4c2ff`](https://github.com/nora953/fawri-production-source/commit/8cd4c2fffe41b020a77bee11fa65aa5f6c8b4026). Existing `scripts/v1-production-media-g5-archive.mjs` opened archives with `O_EXCL` to refuse overwriting a prior encrypted backup, but its catch handler **unconditionally deleted the output path**. A second export against an existing backup could therefore erase the previously accepted ciphertext on expected `EEXIST`. The handler now removes partial files **only when the current invocation successfully created them**; it never unlinks an older backup after a refused create. ## 2026-11-15 — catalog media first-commercial durability admission + cross-merchant physical delete guard (CI pending)

- **Concrete V1 hosting dependency:** `catalogMediaStorage.ts` currently supplies only a `filesystem` media provider, with `getFawriDataDir()` otherwise falling back to app-local `data/`. A commercial container redeploy with that default can lose product images and queued merchant-media deletion manifests. Source [`4317b90b`](https://github.com/nora953/fawri-production-source/commit/4317b90b2cb4429f444247938edb96fb1e37b16d), corrected [`f355e0f0`](https://github.com/nora953/fawri-production-source/commit/f355e0f0de04a2f1089d1f663bd629bbc8d244b6), adds **fail-closed production configuration** `V1_PERSISTENT_CATALOG_MEDIA_ROOT_REQUIRED` if `FAWRI_DATA_DIR` is unset, relative, root, inside process cwd, `/tmp`, `/var/tmp` or `/dev/shm`, or if an unsupported catalog-media provider was selected. Focused negative tests were added. This validates *configuration intent* only: the actual mount, write/restart/read persistence, backups and independent product-photo restore must be proven on the chosen host before launch.
- **Correct storage external blocker scope:** `CATALOG_IMAGE_DURABLE_STORAGE_EXTERNAL_PROOF_REQUIRED` remains mandatory in **manual V1**, where product images ARE offered; the separate `SUPPORT_IMAGE_DURABLE_STORAGE_EXTERNAL_PROOF_REQUIRED` is listed only outside manual V1 because in-app Support is **not offered**. Do not waive catalog-media backup or deletion/retention.
- **Confirmed tenant image deletion weakness:** the post-commit deletion coordinator used database-derived `catalog_image_references.storage_key` to remove files without checking the key's merchant prefix again. A foreign/forged key in a deletion manifest could unlink another merchant's image. Source [`456b42b4`](https://github.com/nora953/fawri-production-source/commit/456b42b4196566cd8b9181b0e93e53b51f169b71), test escape correction [`f6bd857b`](https://github.com/nora953/fawri-production-source/commit/f6bd857ba38afb51cd4cdb1d7c8e4c1a308ffdce): `removeCatalogMediaStorageObject` now requires `merchantId` and applies existing `assertMerchantStorageKey` **before** calling the physical provider; cleanup passes the deleting merchant's ID. ## 2026-11-09 — cashier operator single-primary fail-closed fix (source accepted)

- Found a **second real cashier financial upload logic defect**: active operator `operationKind` previously selected the upload endpoint if it found *any* return/void/sale marker, even when another conflicting primary event was in the same local operation group. This could misclassify damaged local history. ## 2026-11-09 — active cashier ACK exact-snapshot protection

- **New actual P0 source safety defect:** after a valid server ACK, the operator uploader previously called `acknowledgeSynced([operationId])`; the local IndexedDB method deleted every currently stored row with that operation ID. A late or restored row sharing that ID that was never in the ACK could therefore disappear. [Source `5cc9f81f`](https://github.com/nora953/fawri-production-source/commit/5cc9f81f28e2dc9534aac899cea417996875d5df) adds `acknowledgeSyncedExact`: compare complete current transaction-visible entity keys + original payload/count against the actual originally sent snapshots; on mismatch, roll back the single readwrite transaction without any deletion. [Active operator wiring `9a56ffeb`](https://github.com/nora953/fawri-production-source/commit/9a56ffeb833d37c6e8de0300bfcbddcd9da3ad6a) follows validated full ACK only; no provider or protocol v2 changes.
- [Native Chromium/IndexedDB #42702615503](https://github.com/nora953/fawri-production-source/actions/runs/42702615503) **SUCCESS** with `exactSnapshotAckProtection:true` in the actual browser log: created real sale+inventory envelopes, inserted a late same-ID row, proved changed local data was NOT deleted, and proved an exact ACK removes only the fully sent operation leaving later records untouched. [Targeted operator ACK/sequence 37/37 #42702615436](https://github.com/nora953/fawri-production-source/actions/runs/42702615436) **SUCCESS**. [Full quality #42702615806](https://github.com/nora953/fawri-production-source/actions/runs/42702615806) was PENDING when written; verify its exact job result before claiming final green. [Detailed evidence](docs/final-audit/evidence/P0-CASHIER-OPERATOR-EXACT-ACK-LOCAL-SNAPSHOT-20261109.md).
- This does not close `CASHIER-SYNC-SEQUENCE-001`: physical original device stream/restore and independently verified server SQL+HTTP cross-device sequence remain unaccepted, as does optional movement-only v2. External provider/Meta/KMS/hosted identity still tracked, never waived. No user action currently needed for this source fix. 29 CLOSED_SOURCE / 7 OPEN / 9 BLOCKED (historical 29/45 = 64.4% known source-row count, not overall launch readiness), NO-GO, PR #550 draft, no deployment/merge/hosted mutations.

## 2026-11-09 — full operator ACK/restore race and atomic notification deduplication

**Two bounded source/software milestones, no commercial or P0 gate waivers:**

1. The already hardened active cashier operator `acknowledgeSyncedExact` local snapshot guard now has a **real end-to-end native Chromium test** invoking the true `syncCashierOperatorOutboxToCloud` with original native sale/inventory records, a properly bound synthetic operator session, two sequenced local sales and an intercepted **synthetic HTTP ACK only**. While first request is in flight it appends a late same-ID inventory record: real operator drain fails with `CASHIER_LOCAL_ACK_EVIDENCE_MISMATCH`, retains all local records and never uploads the next sequence. ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING` could report `deduplicated:false` for **two simultaneous callers** even though PostgreSQL persisted one row. ON CONFLICT (id) DO NOTHING RETURNING id** inside unchanged trusted merchant scope. [Real PostgreSQL+mounted HTTP #42708081051](https://github.com/nora953/fawri-production-source/actions/runs/42708081051) **SUCCESS**, 8 concurrent calls to two tenant identities => 2 actual inserts, 6 accurate deduplication responses, no cross-merchant HTTP read/write, no replay rewrite. [Scoped evidence](docs/final-audit/evidence/P0-FIELD002-OPERATIONAL-NOTIFICATION-ATOMIC-DEDUPE-20261109.md). This strengthens `P0-RESIDUAL-MERCHANT-PRIVACY-001/http` but does not prove hosted role parity, historic notice provenance or deployment.

## 2026-11-10 — cashier offline payment enum validation (real source defect fixed)

- **Verified current payment/runtime defect**: `commitSale` accepted unexpected untyped `payment_method` and `payment_status` strings, even though TypeScript union declarations list only specific values. A poisoned sale could consume the trusted device sequence, persist an unsyncable immutable outbox and block later checkout. [Source fix 37bac34d](https://github.com/nora953/fawri-production-source/commit/37bac34d0339aa376fa7566219b2129d89e69180) now rejects unknown methods and statuses **before opening any readwrite IndexedDB transaction**; preserves the existing failed-payment denial. Real native Chromium / IndexedDB [#42711439168](https://github.com/nora953/fawri-production-source/actions/runs/42711439168) **COMPLETED SUCCESS**, explicit markers confirm no mutation of meta/stock/sale/movements/outbox, no lost sequence; valid two-SKU stock, replay and conflict cases also PASS. Full [4/4 quality jobs #42711439397](https://github.com/nora953/fawri-production-source/actions/runs/42711439397) **COMPLETED SUCCESS** on the accepted source/test SHA `6f42e6f3`.
- **Corrected earlier hypothesis transparently:** Duplicate product lines are already deliberately rejected in `resolveCashierSalePricing` *before any write*, so a hypothesized double-line oversell **was not a production defect**. Restored original sale stock logic unchanged at [revert a1ecc4ac](https://github.com/nora953/fawri-production-source/commit/a1ecc4ac2cd507201fa4b4cf3297473f6c203a58). The native browser regression now verifies duplicate-SKU denial + zero mutation; erroneous exploratory red tests are **not** accepted. ## 2026-11-10 — FIELD002 subscription notice conflict-safe dedupe, actual PG+HTTP PASS

- **Actual source bug:** Three subscription reminder producers previously used `INSERT ... ON CONFLICT(id) DO NOTHING` and unconditionally advanced their `*_reminder_sent_at` flags even if the globally unique notification ID referred to an unrelated merchant, audience, type or source event. That could silently mark a notice as delivered when none had been recorded for the current merchant. [Source fix `312f9286`](https://github.com/nora953/fawri-production-source/commit/312f9286309a8e96fcc9fc6531297e0a4c4fe499) now checks real `INSERT ... RETURNING id` success, then validates any existing row matches the **same merchant/account, audience, type, title/body template and source entity** under the original restricted merchant SQL scope; otherwise throws `NOTIFICATION_SOURCE_ID_CONFLICT` and rolls back all sent markers. A legitimate same-merchant/event replay preserves the immutable notice.
- [Scoped real PostgreSQL authenticated HTTP run **#42747885868**](https://github.com/nora953/fawri-production-source/actions/runs/42747885868) **COMPLETED SUCCESS** for source SHA `e7941317`, includes fabricated cross-merchant global-ID collision -> 409, zero subscription/batch/notice mutation and cross-tenant read deny, followed by exact own-source replay acceptance and immutable original notification. Log `P0_SUBSCRIPTION_NOTIFICATION_CONFLICT_FAIL_CLOSED` confirms `foreignIdConflict409:true`, `ownSentMarkerPreservedOnError:true`, `exactOwnSourceReplayAccepted:true`, `peerPreserved:true`, `hostedAccepted:false`. [Full build/typecheck/translation/tests **4/4 SUCCESS #42747885973**](https://github.com/nora953/fawri-production-source/actions/runs/42747885973). [Bounded detailed evidence](docs/final-audit/evidence/P0-FIELD002-SUBSCRIPTION-REMINDER-ID-CONFLICT-20261110.md).
- This is **source integrity**, not public exploit proof or hosted provider identity acceptance. FIELD002 and P0-RESIDUAL-MERCHANT-PRIVACY-001/http remain OPEN until separately certified restricted hosted SQL+HTTP identities/actor parity and historical retention. Payment/Meta/KMS/backups and physical cashier device remain EXTERNAL BLOCKED; no owner action needed for this source correction. ## 2026-11-10 — operational payment-conflict notification phantom dedupe blocked

- **Found and fixed a bounded, real source integrity defect** outside the prior retention work: operational merchant notification producer correctly used atomic `INSERT ... ON CONFLICT (id) DO NOTHING RETURNING id`, but treated **every** globally duplicated notification ID as an accepted replay, even if the stored row belonged to another merchant or held altered source/order/payment-conflict fields. This can hide a required payment-conflict notice behind a false `deduplicated:true` response. [Production fix `14c00696`](https://github.com/nora953/fawri-production-source/commit/14c0069648dc6079a4be1af3938062e71c89001e) requires exact existing merchant/account/audience/type/template/source + immutable JSON payload match in the original scoped PostgreSQL transaction; otherwise `OPERATIONAL_NOTIFICATION_SOURCE_CONFLICT` fails closed and preserves historical row. Legitimate same-event replays with later timestamps still succeed without overwrites.
- **Focused real PostgreSQL 16 isolation CI** [#42762113788](https://github.com/nora953/fawri-production-source/actions/runs/42762113788) **COMPLETED SUCCESS**: two merchant identities, synthetic foreign-held globally colliding payment-conflict ID rejected without *any* SQL row mutation, valid original insert + duplicate accepted, changed order under same event rejected, mutated source rejected. Exact marker `P0_OPERATIONAL_NOTIFICATION_DEDUPE_IDENTITY_REAL_SQL` with `actualPostgres:true`, `restrictedMerchantTransaction:true`, `foreignGlobalIdDenied:true`, `noCrossMerchantMutation:true`, `hostedAccepted:false`. No preexisting full role-audit suite was manually repeated; [bounded evidence](docs/final-audit/evidence/P0-FIELD002-OPERATIONAL-NOTIFICATION-FOREIGN-DEDUPE-20261110.md). [Full quality #42762114532](https://github.com/nora953/fawri-production-source/actions/runs/42762114532) was PENDING when initially documented; verify exact job results before claiming.
- **Not a full P0 closure:** full hosted restricted SQL+HTTP identity/field/actor matrix, original approval of historical writer retention and actual signed payment/provider remain separately OPEN/BLOCKED.
